       [data-recaptcha-explicit=\"true\"]></div>\n *\n * The Google enterprise.js script for a given key (and the key-less \"explicit\" mode) is injected\n * at most ONCE per page, regardless of how const locale = languageCode + \"_\" + countryCode.toUpperCase();\n\n          // Call magic link resend API via aemSettings endpoint\n          const baseUrl = aemSettings?.rh_account_creation?.magicLinkSendEndpoint;\n          if (!baseUrl) {\n            console.error(\"resend email endpoint not configured\");\n            return;\n          }\n          const resendUrl = baseUrl + \"?email=\" + encodeURIComponent(email) + \"&locale=\" + encodeURIComponent(locale);\n\n          // Disable the button immediately\n          buttonElML.setAttribute(\"disabled\", \"true\");\n\n          try {\n            const response = await fetch(resendUrl, {\n              method: \"GET\",\n            });\n            if (!response.ok) {\n              throw new Error(`API error: ${response.statusText}`);\n            }\n\n            // Create toast\n            const toast = document.createElement(\"rhcl-toast\");\n            const successToastCopy = centeredContentBlock.dataset.successToastCopy;\n            toast.setAttribute(\"copy\", successToastCopy || \"Confirmation email has been successfully resent.\");\n            toast.setAttribute(\"theme\", \"success\");\n            toast.setAttribute(\"with-icon\", \"true\");\n\n            // Append toast on parent el\n            let parentEl = centeredContentBlock.parentElement;\n            parentEl.appendChild(toast);\n          } catch (e) {\n            console.error(\"failed to resend magic link email: \", e);\n          }\n        });\n      }\n    }\n\n    centeredContentBlocks.forEach((centeredContentBlock) => {\n      //if within modal do not run logic to replace tokens\n      if (!modal?.contains(centeredContentBlock)) {\n        updateTokens(centeredContentBlock);\n      //otherwise if in modal, add a listener to update on successful submission\n      } else {\n          [thankYouXf, accountCreateXf].forEach((el) => {\n            if (el) {\n              el.addEventListener(\"replaceTokens\", () => updateTokens(centeredContentBlock));\n            }\n          });\n      }\n    });\n\n For more information about the First and Third Party Cookies used please follow this link.\n            <br><a href=\"https://www.roberthalf.com/gb/en/website-terms-use#section11_cookies\" class=\"privacy-notice-link\" rel=\"noopener noreferrer\" target=\"_blank\" aria-label=\"More information about your privacy, opens in a new tab\">More information</a></div><button id=\"accept-recommended-btn-handler\" ot-button-order=\"0\">Allow All</button><section class=\"ot-sdk-row ot-cat-grp\"><h3 id=\"ot-category-title\">Manage Consent Preferences</h3><div class=\"ot-accordion-layout ot-cat-item ot-vs-config\" data-optanongroupid=\"1\"><button aria-expanded=\"false\" ot-accordion=\"true\" aria-controls=\"ot-desc-id-1\" aria-labelledby=\"ot-header-id-1 ot-status-id-1\"></button><!-- Accordion header --><div class=\"ot-acc-hdr ot-always-active-group\"><div class=\"ot-plus-minus\"><span></span><span></span></div><h4 class=\"ot-cat-header\" id=\"ot-header-id-1\">Strictly Necessary Cookies</h4><div id=\"ot-status-id-1\" class=\"ot-always-active\">Always Active</div></div><!-- accordion detail --><div class=\"ot-acc-grpcntr ot-acc-txt\"><p class=\"ot-acc-grpdesc ot-category-desc\" id=\"ot-desc-id-1\">Strictly necessary cookies are always active as they enable core functionality of the website such as user logins, account management, privacy preferences, completing forms and moving back and forth between websites without losing previous actions. Strictly necessary cookies are exempt from collecting user consent because they are necessary for the website to work properly and for security purposes. We cannot switch off essential cookies in our systems, but you can set your browser to block these cookies or to notify you about these cookies. If you choose to block strictly necessary cookies, some areas of the website may not function. These cookies generally do not store any personal data, unless this is required to provide the service requested such as when users log into our website. All other cookies and tracking technologies not classified as strictly necessary are subject to prior consent from website visitors.\n\n</p></div></div><div class=\"ot-accordion-layout ot-cat-item ot-vs-config\" data-optanongroupid=\"C0003\"><button aria-expanded=\"false\" ot-accordion=\"true\" aria-controls=\"ot-desc-id-C0003\" aria-labelledby=\"ot-header-id-C0003\"></button><!-- Accordion header --><div class=\"ot-acc-hdr\"><div class=\"ot-plus-minus\"><span></span><span></span></div><h4 class=\"ot-cat-header\" id=\"ot-header-id-C0003\">Functional Cookies</h4><div class=\"ot-tgl\"><input type=\"checkbox\" name=\"ot-group-id-C0003\" id=\"ot-group-id-C0003\" role=\"switch\" class=\"category-switch-handler\" data-optanongroupid=\"C0003\" checked=\"\" aria-labelledby=\"ot-header-id-C0003\"> <label class=\"ot-switch\" for=\"ot-group-id-C0003\"><span class=\"ot-switch-nob\"></span> <span class=\"ot-label-txt\">Functional Cookies</span></label> </div></div><!-- accordion detail --><div class=\"ot-acc-grpcntr ot-acc-txt\"><p class=\"ot-acc-grpdesc ot-category-desc\" id=\"ot-desc-id-C0003\">Functional cookies enable the website to provide enhanced functionality and personalization. These cookies support features that you actively chose to use such as integrated third-party services (e.g., Apply with LinkedIn),  remembering user preferences, saved account settings, or provided customized content and features. They may be set by us (first-party cookies) or by third-party providers whose services we have embedded on our pages (LinkedIn, YouTube). These providers may use browser/user ids, cookies or similar technologies to ensure that their services operate correctly within your browser environment, maintain session continuity, support secure interactions, and deliver requested functionality.\n\nFunctional cookies, web beacons/pixel tags and similar tracking technologies require your consent before they are placed. If you do not consent to the placement of these cookies and similar technologies, these services may not function properly. This may include the inability to use integrated applications tools (Apply with Linkedin), personalized settings, embedded media, or other enhanced website features.\n\nYou can manage or withdraw your consent for functional cookies at any time through the Cookie Preference Center by clicking the green symbol on the bottom left-hand side of the website.</p></div></div><div class=\"ot-accordion-layout ot-cat-item ot-vs-config\" data-optanongroupid=\"2\"><button aria-expanded=\"false\" ot-accordion=\"true\" aria-controls=\"ot-desc-id-2\" aria-labelledby=\"ot-header-id-2\"></button><!-- Accordion header --><div class=\"ot-acc-hdr\"><div class=\"ot-plus-minus\"><span></span><span></span></div><h4 class=\"ot-cat-header\" id=\"ot-header-id-2\">Performance Cookies and Web Beacons/Pixel Tags</h4><div class=\"ot-tgl\"><input type=\"checkbox\" name=\"ot-group-id-2\" id=\"ot-group-id-2\" role=\"switch\" class=\"category-switch-handler\" data-optanongroupid=\"2\" checked=\"\" aria-labelledby=\"ot-header-id-2\"> <label class=\"ot-switch\" for=\"ot-group-id-2\"><span class=\"ot-switch-nob\"></span> <span class=\"ot-label-txt\">Performance Cookies and Web Beacons/Pixel Tags</span></label> </div></div><!-- accordion detail --><div class=\"ot-acc-grpcntr ot-acc-txt\"><p class=\"ot-acc-grpdesc ot-category-desc\" id=\"ot-desc-id-2\">Our performance cookies, web beacon/pixel tags and other tracking technologies generally do not store any personal data. We track user interaction with the site to enhance website functionality and content. We generally use the information tracked through these cookies, web beacon/pixel tags and other tracking technologies in an aggregated and unidentifiable format. However, if you have submitted a form or applied for a job, a visitor ID will be associated with your submitted information and can be used to identify yourself and your website behavior. If you need more details about this visitor ID matching, refer to our Privacy Notice.  \nRejecting the placement of performance cookies will result in performance cookies and tags being blocked which will not allow the cookies, web beacons/pixels and other tracking technologies to track your website behavior or set the visitor ID.</p></div></div><div class=\"ot-accordion-layout ot-cat-item ot-vs-config\" data-optanongroupid=\"C008\"><button aria-expanded=\"false\" ot-accordion=\"true\" aria-controls=\"ot-desc-id-C008\" aria-labelledby=\"ot-header-id-C008\"></button><!-- Accordion header --><div class=\"ot-acc-hdr\"><div class=\"ot-plus-minus\"><span></span><span></span></div><h4 class=\"ot-cat-header\" id=\"ot-header-id-C008\">Advertising and Social Media Cookies and Web Beacons/Pixel Tags</h4><div class=\"ot-tgl\"><input type=\"checkbox\" name=\"ot-group-id-C008\" id=\"ot-group-id-C008\" role=\"switch\" class=\"category-switch-handler\" data-optanongroupid=\"C008\" checked=\"\" aria-labelledby=\"ot-header-id-C008\"> <label class=\"ot-switch\" for=\"ot-group-id-C008\"><span class=\"ot-switch-nob\"></span> <span class=\"ot-label-txt\">Advertising and Social Media Cookies and Web Beacons/Pixel Tags</span></label> </div></div><!-- accordion detail --><div class=\"ot-acc-grpcntr ot-acc-txt\"><p class=\"ot-acc-grpdesc ot-category-desc\" id=\"ot-desc-id-C008\">These cookies, web beacons/pixel tags, and other tracking technologies allow us to collect data on unique user identifiers, device information, technical details, browsing behavior, and preferences, enabling us to tailor advertising to better align with your interests.
